## Section 6 — Trade × orderbook snapshot cross-reference

Matched trades (±30s, book available): **4,348** / 93,838

| Classification | Count | % of matched |
|---|---:|---:|
| at bid | 235 | 5.4% |

| _no book match_ | 89,490 | — |

Trades landing at the bid or ask cost one tick of spread; 'mid' trades execute between the posted NBBO (e.g., price improvement from a hidden maker).

## Section 7 — Sizing implications

- Trades ≥ 100 contracts: **33.8%** of all trades (31,742/93,838).
- Trades ≥ 500 contracts: **10.5%** of all trades (9,852/93,838).
- Trades ≥ 1000 contracts: **5.7%** of all trades (5,381/93,838).
- Percentile of a 100-contract order: roughly the **66th** percentile (median trade = 45.0 contracts).

- In-game 5-min buckets where a 100-contract order is <1% of bucket volume: **100%** of buckets.
- Median in-game 5-min bucket volume: **574,403** contracts → a 100-contract order is ~**0.02%** of that bucket.
